Table 3:
Stability properties of the critical point
(x_{\psi,c},x_{V,c},x_{W,c})
based on the three eigenvalues
\mu_{1},\mu_{2},
and
\mu_{3}
.

In this Appendix we consider the linear perturbation of dynamical equations (
57
)-(
59
) in the critical points
(x_{\psi,c},x_{V,c},x_{W,c})
. First, we expand autonomous variables around these points

\displaystyle x_{\psi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle x_{\psi,c}+u_{\psi},

(113)

\displaystyle x_{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle x_{V,c}+u_{V},

(114)

\displaystyle x_{W}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle x_{W,c}+u_{W}.

(115)

The first order equation of motions has the form

\displaystyle\frac{2u_{\psi}^{\prime}}{D-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[3(1-w_{m})x_{\psi,c}^{2}-(1+w_{m})x_{V,c}^{2}-(f_{2}(u)+w_{m}f_{1}(u))x_{W,c}^{2}-(1-w_{m})\Big]u_{\psi}

\displaystyle-\,\Bigg[2(1+w_{m})x_{\psi,c}-4\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\lambda_{V}\Bigg]x_{V,c}u_{V}-\Bigg[2(f_{2}(u)+w_{m}f_{1}(u))x_{\psi,c}-4\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\lambda_{W}f_{1}(u)\Bigg]x_{W,c}u_{W},

\displaystyle\frac{2u_{V}^{\prime}}{D-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg[2(1-w_{m})x_{\psi,c}-\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\lambda_{V}\Bigg]x_{V,c}u_{\psi}+\Bigg[(1+w_{m})+(1-w_{m})x_{\psi,c}^{2}

\displaystyle-\,3(1+w_{m})x_{V,c}^{2}-(f_{2}(u)+w_{m}f_{1}(u))x_{W,c}^{2}-\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\lambda_{V}x_{\psi,c}\Bigg]u_{V}-2(f_{2}(u)+w_{m}f_{1}(u))x_{V,c}x_{W,c}u_{W},

\displaystyle\frac{2u_{W}^{\prime}}{D-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg[2(1-w_{m})x_{\psi,c}-\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\lambda_{W}\Bigg]x_{W,c}u_{\psi}-2(1+w_{m})x_{V,c}x_{W,c}u_{V}

(118)

\displaystyle+\,\Bigg[(1+w_{m})+(1-w_{m})x_{\psi,c}^{2}-(1+w_{m})x_{V,c}^{2}-3(f_{2}(u)+w_{m}f_{1}(u))x_{W,c}^{2}-\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\lambda_{W}x_{\psi,c}\Bigg]u_{W},

such that we can cast the above equations into the matrix form

\begin{pmatrix}u_{\psi}^{\prime}\\
u_{V}^{\prime}\\
u_{W}^{\prime}\end{pmatrix}=\bm{J}\begin{pmatrix}u_{\psi}\\
u_{V}\\
u_{W}\end{pmatrix},

(119)

where
\bm{J}
is Jacobian matrix. Let the eigenvalues of
\bm{J}
are
\mu_{1}
,
\mu_{2}
, and
\mu_{3}
. We write the Jacobian matrix
\bm{J}
and its eigenvalues for each of the critical points below. These then can be used to analyze their stability properties, which we summarize in Table
3
.

•

The Jacobian matrix for CP
1
:
(0,0,0)
is

\bm{J}=\begin{pmatrix}-(1-w_{m})&0&0\\
0&1+w_{m}&0\\
0&0&1+w_{m}\end{pmatrix},

(120)

with eigenvalues

\{-(1-w_{m}),1+w_{m}\}.

(121)

•

The Jacobian matrix for CP
2
:
(1,0,0)
is

\bm{J}=\begin{pmatrix}2(1-w_{m})&0&0\\
0&2-\lambda_{V}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}&0\\
0&0&2-\lambda_{W}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\end{pmatrix},

(122)

with eigenvalues

\Bigg\{2(1-w_{m}),2-\lambda_{V}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}},2-\lambda_{W}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\Bigg\}.

(123)

•

The Jacobian matrix for CP
3
:
(-1,0,0)
is

\bm{J}=\begin{pmatrix}2(1-w_{m})&0&0\\
0&2+\lambda_{V}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}&0\\
0&0&2+\lambda_{W}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\end{pmatrix},

(124)

with eigenvalues

\Bigg\{2(1-w_{m}),2+\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}},2+\lambda_{W}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\Bigg\}.

(125)

•

The Jacobian matrix for CP
4
:
(x_{\psi,c},0,0)
is

\bm{J}=\begin{pmatrix}0&0&0\\
0&2-\lambda_{V}x_{\psi,c}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}&0\\
0&0&2-\lambda_{W}x_{\psi,c}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\end{pmatrix},

(126)

with eigenvalues

\Bigg\{0,2-\lambda_{V}x_{\psi,c}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}},2-\lambda_{W}x_{\psi,c}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}}\Bigg\}.

(127)

•

One of the eigenvalues of the Jacobian matrix for CP
5
:
\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\pm}}{\lambda_{V}}\sqrt{\frac{D-1}{D-2}},\frac{1}{\lambda_{V}}\sqrt{\frac{(D-1)(2-\mathcal{A}_{\pm})\mathcal{A}_{\pm}}{2(D-2)}},0\bigg)
is

\mu_{1}=\left(1-\frac{\lambda_{W}}{\lambda_{V}}\right)\mathcal{A}_{\pm}.

(128)

•

The Jacobian matrix for CP
6
:
\bigg(0,\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{W}}{\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V}}},\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{V}}{|f_{1}(u)|(\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V})}}\bigg)
is

\bm{J}=\begin{pmatrix}-2&-4\lambda_{V}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}\frac{\lambda_{W}}{\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V}}}&4\lambda_{W}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}\frac{|f_{1}(u)|\lambda_{V}}{\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V}}}\\
-\lambda_{V}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}\frac{\lambda_{W}}{\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V}}}&-\frac{2(1+w_{m})\lambda_{W}}{\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V}}&-\frac{2(1+w_{m})\sqrt{|f_{1}(u)|\lambda_{V}\lambda_{W}}}{\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V}}\\
-\lambda_{W}\sqrt{\frac{D-2}{D-1}\frac{\lambda_{V}}{|f_{1}(u)|(\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V})}}&-\frac{2(1+w_{m})}{\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{V}\lambda_{W}}{|f_{1}(u)|}}&\frac{2(1+w_{m})\lambda_{V}}{\lambda_{W}-\lambda_{V}}\end{pmatrix},

(129)

with eigenvalues

\Bigg\{-2(1+w_{m}),-1\pm\sqrt{1-\frac{4(D-1)\lambda_{V}\lambda_{W}}{D-2}}\Bigg\}.

(130)

•

The Jacobian matrix for CP
7
:
\bigg(0,\sqrt{1-f_{1}(u)x_{W,c}^{2}},x_{W,c}\bigg)
is

\bm{J}=\begin{pmatrix}-2&0&0\\
0&-2(1+w_{m})(1-f_{1}(u)x_{W,c}^{2})&-2(1+w_{m})f_{1}(u)x_{W,c}\sqrt{1-f_{1}(u)x_{W,c}^{2}}\\
0&-2(1+w_{m})x_{W,c}\sqrt{1-f_{1}(u)x_{W,c}^{2}}&-2(1+w_{m})f_{1}(u)x_{W,x}^{2}\end{pmatrix},

(131)

with eigenvalues

\{-2,-2(1+w_{m}),0\}.

(132)

•

One of the eigenvalues of the Jacobian matrix for CP
8
:
\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{B}_{\pm}}{\lambda_{W}}\sqrt{\frac{D-1}{D-2}},0,\frac{1}{\lambda_{W}}\sqrt{\frac{(D-1)(2-\mathcal{B}_{\pm})\mathcal{B}_{\pm}}{2(D-2)f_{1}(u)}}\bigg)
is

\mu_{1}=\left(1-\frac{\lambda_{V}}{\lambda_{W}}\right)\mathcal{B}_{\pm}.

(133)
